import "@/App.css";
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route, Navigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { Toaster } from "sonner";
import { AppProvider } from "@/context/AppContext";
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from "@/context/AuthContext";
import Login from "@/pages/Login";
import Sidebar from "@/components/layout/Sidebar";
import Topbar from "@/components/layout/Topbar";
import CustomerSupport from "@/pages/CustomerSupport";
import Operations from "@/pages/Operations";
import FrontendOps from "@/pages/FrontendOps";
import Finance from "@/pages/Finance";
import Admin from "@/pages/Admin";
import AiAssistant from "@/pages/AiAssistant";

function Shell() {
  return (
    <AppProvider>
      <div className="flex h-screen w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-50 text-slate-900">
        <Sidebar />
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">
          <Topbar />
          <main className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden">
            <Routes>
              <Route path="/" element={<Navigate to="/assistant" replace />} />
              <Route path="/assistant" element={<AiAssistant />} />
              <Route path="/support" element={<CustomerSupport />} />
              <Route path="/operations" element={<Operations />} />
              <Route path="/frontend-ops" element={<FrontendOps />} />
              <Route path="/finance" element={<Finance />} />
              <Route path="/admin" element={<Admin />} />
            </Routes>
          </main>
        </div>
      </div>
    </AppProvider>
  );
}

function Gate() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  if (user === null) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-screen w-full items-center justify-center bg-slate-50">
        <div className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-slate-300 border-t-slate-900" />
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (!user) return <Login />;
  return <Shell />;
}

function App() {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <BrowserRouter>
        <Gate />
        <Toaster position="top-right" richColors />
      </BrowserRouter>
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}

export default App;


======================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/AiAssistPanel.jsx
======================================================================

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { Sparkles, Loader2, ClipboardCheck, ListChecks, X } from "lucide-react";
import { SentimentBadge } from "@/components/shared/UI";
import { toast } from "sonner";

const COLLAPSE = { initial: { opacity: 0, height: 0 }, animate: { opacity: 1, height: "auto" }, exit: { opacity: 0, height: 0 } };
const FADE_UP = { initial: { opacity: 0, y: 8 }, animate: { opacity: 1, y: 0 } };

export default function AiAssistPanel({ ticketId, chatId, onApply }) {
  const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState([]);
  const [summary, setSummary] = useState(null);
  const [loadingS, setLoadingS] = useState(false);
  const [loadingSum, setLoadingSum] = useState(false);

  const getSuggestions = async () => {
    setLoadingS(true);
    try {
      const { data } = await api.aiReplySuggestions({ ticket_id: ticketId, chat_id: chatId });
      setSuggestions(data.suggestions || []);
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error("AI suggestion failed. Check LLM key balance.");
    } finally {
      setLoadingS(false);
    }
  };

  const getSummary = async () => {
    setLoadingSum(true);
    try {
      const { data } = await api.aiChatSummary({ ticket_id: ticketId, chat_id: chatId });
      setSummary(data);
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error("AI summary failed. Check LLM key balance.");
    } finally {
      setLoadingSum(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4" data-testid="ai-assist-panel">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <div className="rounded-lg bg-blue-50 p-1.5 text-blue-600"><Sparkles size={15} /></div>
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">AI Assist</p>
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <button
          data-testid="ai-generate-suggestions-btn"
          onClick={getSuggestions}
          disabled={loadingS}
          className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {loadingS ? <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : <ListChecks size={13} />} Suggest replies
        </button>
        <button
          data-testid="ai-generate-summary-btn"
          onClick={getSummary}
          disabled={loadingSum}
          className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {loadingSum ? <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : <ClipboardCheck size={13} />} Summarize
        </button>
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence>
        {summary && (
          <motion.div
            {...COLLAPSE}
            className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50/50 p-3"
            data-testid="ai-chat-summary-box"
          >
            <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">Conversation summary</p>
              <button onClick={() => setSummary(null)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600"><X size={13} /></button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-700">{summary.summary}</p>
            {summary.points?.length > 0 && (
              <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1">
                {summary.points.map((pt, i) => (
                  <li key={`pt-${i}`} className="flex gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600"><span className="text-blue-500">•</span>{pt}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2">
              <SentimentBadge sentiment={summary.sentiment} />
              {summary.next && <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Next: {summary.next}</span>}
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        {suggestions.map((s, i) => (
          <motion.div
            key={`sug-${i}`}
            {...FADE_UP}
            transition={{ delay: i * 0.06 }}
            className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-3"
            data-testid="ai-reply-suggestion-card"
          >
            <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-700">{s}</p>
            <button
              data-testid="ai-apply-suggestion-btn"
              onClick={() => { onApply?.(s); toast.success("Reply drafted from AI suggestion"); }}
              className="mt-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-slate-700"
            >
              Use this reply
            </button>
          </motion.div>
        ))}
        {suggestions.length === 0 && !loadingS && (
          <p className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 p-4 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
            Generate smart replies & summaries for this conversation.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


======================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/ai/AiEmail.jsx
======================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { useApp } from "@/context/AppContext";
import { Avatar, MarketplaceBadge, StatusBadge, Pill, EmptyState } from "@/components/shared/UI";
import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/constants";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Bot, Sparkles, Send, Loader2, Mail, CheckCircle2, PencilLine, Zap } from "lucide-react";

function confTone(c) {
  if (c == null) return "text-slate-500";
  if (c >= 0.7) return "text-emerald-600";
  if (c >= 0.45) return "text-amber-600";
  return "text-rose-600";
}

const RESULT_MOTION = { initial: { opacity: 0, y: 10 }, animate: { opacity: 1, y: 0 } };

function emailBubbleClass(isNote, isCustomer, isAi) {
  if (isNote) return "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50";
  if (isCustomer) return "border-slate-200 bg-white";
  if (isAi) return "border-transparent bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 text-white";
  return "border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 text-white";
}

export default function AiEmail() {
  const { currentAgent } = useApp();
  const [tickets, setTickets] = useState([]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null);
  const [processing, setProcessing] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState(null);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("");

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data } = await api.getTickets({ status: "all" });
    setTickets(data);
    setSelected((prev) => (prev ? data.find((t) => t.id === prev.id) || data[0] : data[0]) || null);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);
  useEffect(() => { setResult(null); setDraft(""); }, [selected?.id]);

  const refresh = async (id) => { const { data } = await api.getTicket(id); setSelected(data); setTickets((p) => p.map((t) => (t.id === id ? data : t))); };

  const process = async () => {
    if (!selected) return;
    setProcessing(true); setResult(null);
    try {
      const { data } = await api.aiProcessTicket(selected.id);
      setResult(data);
      setSelected(data.ticket); setTickets((p) => p.map((t) => (t.id === data.ticket.id ? data.ticket : t)));
      if (data.mode === "sent") toast.success("AI auto-sent a reply to the customer");
      else { setDraft(data.draft); toast.info("AI drafted a reply for your review"); }
    } finally { setProcessing(false); }
  };

  const approveDraft = async () => {
    await api.replyTicket(selected.id, { body: draft, mode: "reply", agent_name: currentAgent?.name || "Agent" });
    setResult(null); setDraft("");
    await refresh(selected.id);
    toast.success("Sent — AI learned from your final version");
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid h-full grid-cols-[320px_1fr] overflow-hidden">
      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col border-r border-slate-200 bg-white">
        <div className="border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3"><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">Email tickets</p><p className="text-xs text-slate-400">AI auto-sends simple replies · drafts complex ones</p></div>
        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {tickets.map((t) => {
            const active = selected?.id === t.id;
            return (
              <button key={t.id} data-testid="ai-email-item" onClick={() => setSelected(t)} className={`flex w-full flex-col gap-1 border-b border-slate-100 px-4 py-3 text-left transition-colors ${active ? "bg-indigo-50/70" : "hover:bg-slate-50"}`}>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between"><span className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">{t.from_name}</span><span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">{timeAgo(t.created_at)}</span></div>
                <p className="truncate text-xs text-slate-500">{t.subject}</p>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><MarketplaceBadge name={t.marketplace} />{t.ai_handled && <Pill className="border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700"><Bot size={10} /> AI replied</Pill>}</div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col bg-slate-50/60">
        {selected ? (
          <>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 bg-white/80 px-6 py-4 backdrop-blur">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h3 className="truncate text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{selected.subject}</h3>
                <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500"><Mail size={12} /> {selected.from_email} <StatusBadge status={selected.status} /></p>
              </div>
              <button data-testid="ai-process-btn" onClick={process} disabled={processing} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white shadow-sm transition-transform hover:scale-[1.03] disabled:opacity-60">
                {processing ? <Loader2 size={15} className="animate-spin" /> : <Sparkles size={15} />} Let AI handle this
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-6">
              {selected.messages.map((m) => {
                if (m.author_type === "system") return <div key={m.id} className="text-center"><Pill className="border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-500 normal-case">{m.body}</Pill></div>;
                const isCustomer = m.author_type === "customer";
                const isNote = m.author_type === "note";
                const isAi = m.ai;
                return (
                  <div key={m.id} className={`flex ${isCustomer ? "justify-start" : "justify-end"}`}>
                    <div className={`max-w-[78%] rounded-2xl border p-4 ${emailBubbleClass(isNote, isCustomer, isAi)}`}>
                      <p className={`mb-1 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold ${isCustomer || isNote ? "text-slate-700" : "text-white/90"}`}>{isAi && <Bot size={11} />}{m.author_name}</p>
                      <p className={`whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-relaxed ${isCustomer || isNote ? "text-slate-700" : "text-white"}`}>{m.body}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}

              {result && (
                <motion.div {...RESULT_MOTION} data-testid="ai-email-result" className="rounded-2xl border border-indigo-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm">
                  <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
                      {result.mode === "sent" ? <><Zap size={15} className="text-emerald-600" /> Auto-sent by AI</> : <><PencilLine size={15} className="text-amber-600" /> AI draft — needs your review</>}
                    </p>
                    <span className={`text-xs font-semibold ${confTone(result.confidence)}`}>{Math.round((result.confidence || 0) * 100)}% confidence</span>
                  </div>
                  {result.canned_used && <p className="mb-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400">Based on canned response: <span className="font-medium text-slate-600">{result.canned_used}</span></p>}
                  {result.mode === "draft" ? (
                    <>
                      <textarea data-testid="ai-draft-textarea" value={draft} onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)} rows={6} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-400" />
                      <div className="mt-2 flex justify-end gap-2">
                        <button onClick={() => { setResult(null); setDraft(""); }} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50">Discard</button>
                        <button data-testid="approve-draft-btn" onClick={approveDraft} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-white hover:bg-slate-700"><Send size={13} /> Approve & send</button>
                      </div>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-emerald-600"><CheckCircle2 size={14} /> Reply delivered to the customer and added to the thread above.</p>
                  )}
                </motion.div>
              )}
            </div>
          </>
        ) : <EmptyState icon={Mail} title="Select a ticket" subtitle="Pick an email and let the AI draft or send a reply." />}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


======================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/ai/AiLiveChat.jsx
======================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { useApp } from "@/context/AppContext";
import { Avatar, MarketplaceBadge, SentimentBadge, Pill, EmptyState } from "@/components/shared/UI";
import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/constants";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import {
  Bot, Send, Plus, UserRound, Sparkles, ArrowRightLeft, Hand, Gauge, GraduationCap, ShieldQuestion, Frown, User2,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogFooter } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";

const MKTS = ["Amazon", "Flipkart", "Meesho", "Myntra"];
const QUICK = ["hello", "when will I get my refund?", "my withdrawal failed", "I want to talk to a human agent", "this is ridiculous, my refund is stuck!!"];
const THINKING_MOTION = { initial: { opacity: 0, y: 6 }, animate: { opacity: 1, y: 0 }, exit: { opacity: 0 } };
const DOT_ANIM = { opacity: [0.3, 1, 0.3] };

function confTone(c) {
  if (c == null) return { c: "text-slate-500", bar: "bg-slate-300" };
  if (c >= 0.7) return { c: "text-emerald-600", bar: "bg-emerald-500" };
  if (c >= 0.45) return { c: "text-amber-600", bar: "bg-amber-500" };
  return { c: "text-rose-600", bar: "bg-rose-500" };
}

function chatBubbleClass(isCustomer, isAi) {
  if (isCustomer) return "rounded-tl-sm border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700";
  if (isAi) return "rounded-tr-sm bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-indigo-500/20";
  return "rounded-tr-sm bg-slate-900 text-white";
}

export default function AiLiveChat() {
  const { currentAgent, agents } = useApp();
  const [chats, setChats] = useState([]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null);
  const [speakAs, setSpeakAs] = useState("customer");
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [thinking, setThinking] = useState(false);
  const [aiMeta, setAiMeta] = useState(null);
  const [newOpen, setNewOpen] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ customer_name: "", marketplace: "Amazon", message: "hello" });
  const endRef = useRef(null);

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    const { data } = await api.getChats();
    setChats(data);
    setSelected((prev) => (prev ? data.find((c) => c.id === prev.id) || prev : data.find((c) => c.handled_by === "ai") || data[0]) || null);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);
  useEffect(() => { if (selected) setSpeakAs(selected.ai_active ? "customer" : "agent"); }, [selected?.id, selected?.ai_active]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  useEffect(() => { endRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" }); }, [selected?.messages?.length, thinking]);

  const upsert = (chat) => { setSelected(chat); setChats((prev) => { const has = prev.find((c) => c.id === chat.id); return has ? prev.map((c) => (c.id === chat.id ? chat : c)) : [chat, ...prev]; }); };

  const send = async () => {
    if (!input.trim() || !selected) return;
    const text = input; setInput("");
    if (speakAs === "customer") {
      const optimistic = { ...selected, messages: [...selected.messages, { id: "tmp", author_type: "customer", author_name: selected.customer_name, body: text, created_at: new Date().toISOString() }] };
      setSelected(optimistic);
      setThinking(true);
      try {
        const { data } = await api.customerMessage(selected.id, { body: text });
        upsert(data.chat); setAiMeta(data.ai);
        if (data.ai?.handoff) { toast.warning("AI handed off to a human agent"); setSpeakAs("agent"); }
      } finally { setThinking(false); }
    } else {
      const { data } = await api.sendChatMessage(selected.id, { body: text, agent_name: currentAgent?.name || "Agent" });
      upsert(data);
      toast.success("Sent — AI captured this reply to learn from");
    }
  };

  const createChat = async () => {
    if (!form.customer_name.trim()) return;
    setThinking(true);
    try {
      const { data } = await api.startChat(form);
      setNewOpen(false); setForm({ customer_name: "", marketplace: "Amazon", message: "hello" });
      upsert(data.chat); setAiMeta(data.ai);
    } finally { setThinking(false); }
  };

  const takeover = async () => {
    const { data } = await api.assignChat(selected.id, currentAgent.id);
    upsert(data); setSpeakAs("agent");
    toast.success(`${currentAgent.name} took over from AI`);
  };
  const resumeAi = async () => {
    const { data } = await api.resumeAi(selected.id);
    upsert(data); setSpeakAs("customer");
    toast.success("Fayr Assistant resumed this chat");
  };
  const handoff = async () => {
    const { data } = await api.chatHandoff(selected.id, "Manual handoff by operator");
    upsert(data); setSpeakAs("agent");
    toast.warning("Handed off to human queue");
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid h-full grid-cols-[300px_1fr_300px] overflow-hidden">
      {/* list */}
      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col border-r border-slate-200 bg-white">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 px-4 py-3">
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">Conversations</p>
          <button data-testid="new-chat-btn" onClick={() => setNewOpen(true)} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white shadow-sm transition-transform hover:scale-[1.03]">
            <Plus size={13} /> Simulate
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {chats.length === 0 && <EmptyState icon={Bot} title="No chats yet" subtitle="Click Simulate to start a customer chat." />}
          {chats.map((c) => {
            const active = selected?.id === c.id;
            return (
              <button key={c.id} data-testid="ai-chat-item" onClick={() => setSelected(c)} className={`flex w-full items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 px-4 py-3 text-left transition-colors ${active ? "bg-indigo-50/70" : "hover:bg-slate-50"}`}>
                <Avatar name={c.customer_name} size={36} />
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
                    <span className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">{c.customer_name}</span>
                    <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] text-slate-400">{timeAgo(c.last_at)}</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="truncate text-xs text-slate-500">{c.messages[c.messages.length - 1]?.body}</p>
                  <div className="mt-1">
                    {c.ai_active ? (
                      <Pill className="border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700"><Bot size={10} /> AI handling</Pill>
                    ) : (
                      <Pill className="border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600"><User2 size={10} /> Human</Pill>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* conversation */}
      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col bg-slate-50/60">
        {selected ? (
          <>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 bg-white/80 px-6 py-3 backdrop-blur">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Avatar name={selected.customer_name} size={38} />
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">{selected.customer_name}</p>
                  <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500"><MarketplaceBadge name={selected.marketplace} /> · started {timeAgo(selected.started_at)}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {selected.ai_active ? (
                  <>
                    <Pill className="border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700"><Bot size={11} /> AI active</Pill>
                    <button data-testid="chat-handoff-btn" onClick={handoff} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"><Hand size={13} /> Hand off</button>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <button data-testid="chat-takeover-btn" onClick={takeover} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-100"><UserRound size={13} /> Take over</button>
                    <button data-testid="chat-resume-ai-btn" onClick={resumeAi} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-indigo-700 hover:bg-indigo-100"><Bot size={13} /> Resume AI</button>
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {!selected.ai_active && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-6 py-2.5 text-xs text-amber-800" data-testid="handoff-banner">
                <ArrowRightLeft size={14} /> {selected.handoff_reason ? `Handed to a human — ${selected.handoff_reason}.` : "This chat is being handled by a human agent."}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto p-6">
              {selected.messages.map((m) => {
                if (m.author_type === "system") return <div key={m.id} className="text-center"><Pill className="border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-500 normal-case">{m.body}</Pill></div>;
                const isCustomer = m.author_type === "customer";
                const isAi = m.ai;
                return (
                  <div key={m.id} className={`flex ${isCustomer ? "justify-start" : "justify-end"}`}>
                    <div className={`max-w-[72%] rounded-2xl px-4 py-2.5 ${chatBubbleClass(isCustomer, isAi)}`}>
                      {!isCustomer && <p className={`mb-0.5 flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-semibold ${isAi ? "text-indigo-100" : "text-slate-300"}`}>{isAi ? <><Bot size={10} /> Fayr Assistant</> : m.author_name}</p>}
                      <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed">{m.body}</p>
                      <p className={`mt-1 text-[10px] ${isCustomer ? "text-slate-400" : "text-white/60"}`}>{timeAgo(m.created_at)}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
              <AnimatePresence>
                {thinking && (
                  <motion.div {...THINKING_MOTION} className="flex justify-end">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl rounded-tr-sm bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 px-4 py-3 shadow-md">
                      {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => <motion.span key={i} className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-white/80" animate={DOT_ANIM} transition={{ duration: 1, repeat: Infinity, delay: i * 0.2 }} />)}
                    </div>
                  </motion.div>
                )}
              </AnimatePresence>
              <div ref={endRef} />
            </div>

            <div className="border-t border-slate-200 bg-white p-4">
              <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-1">
                <span className="mr-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">Speak as</span>
                <button data-testid="speak-as-customer" onClick={() => setSpeakAs("customer")} className={`rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${speakAs === "customer" ? "bg-slate-900 text-white" : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600"}`}>Customer</button>
                <button data-testid="speak-as-agent" onClick={() => setSpeakAs("agent")} className={`rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${speakAs === "agent" ? "bg-slate-900 text-white" : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600"}`}>Agent</button>
                {speakAs === "customer" && selected.ai_active && <span className="ml-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-indigo-500"><Sparkles size={11} /> AI will auto-reply</span>}
              </div>
              {speakAs === "customer" && (
                <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  {QUICK.map((q) => <button key={q} onClick={() => setInput(q)} className="rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100">{q}</button>)}
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <input
                  data-testid="ai-chat-input"
                  value={input}
                  onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && !thinking && send()}
                  placeholder={speakAs === "customer" ? "Type a message as the customer…" : "Reply as a human agent…"}
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none transition-colors focus:border-indigo-400"
                />
                <button data-testid="ai-chat-send" onClick={send} disabled={thinking || !input.trim()} className="rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 p-2.5 text-white transition-transform hover:scale-105 disabled:opacity-50">
                  <Send size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : (
          <EmptyState icon={Bot} title="Start a conversation" subtitle="Click Simulate to see the AI greet and help a customer." />
        )}
      </div>

      {/* right rail */}
      <div className="min-h-0 overflow-y-auto border-l border-slate-200 bg-white p-5">
        <div className="space-y-5">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-indigo-100 bg-gradient-to-b from-indigo-50/60 to-white p-4">
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800"><Gauge size={14} className="text-indigo-600" /> Last AI decision</p>
            {aiMeta ? (
              <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
                <div>
                  <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between text-xs"><span className="text-slate-500">Confidence</span><span className={`font-semibold ${confTone(aiMeta.confidence).c}`}>{Math.round((aiMeta.confidence || 0) * 100)}%</span></div>
                  <div className="h-2 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100"><motion.div className={`h-full ${confTone(aiMeta.confidence).bar}`} initial={{ width: 0 }} animate={{ width: `${(aiMeta.confidence || 0) * 100}%` }} /></div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-600">Sentiment <SentimentBadge sentiment={aiMeta.sentiment} /></div>
                {aiMeta.handoff && <p className="rounded-lg bg-amber-50 p-2 text-[11px] text-amber-800">Handed off: {aiMeta.reason}</p>}
              </div>
            ) : <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-400">Send a message to see the AI's live confidence & sentiment read.</p>}
          </div>

          <div>
            <p className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800"><ArrowRightLeft size={14} className="text-slate-500" /> Handoff triggers</p>
            <ul className="space-y-2 text-xs text-slate-600">
              <li className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 p-2.5"><UserRound size={14} className="text-slate-400" /> User asks for a human agent</li>
              <li className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 p-2.5"><ShieldQuestion size={14} className="text-slate-400" /> AI confidence below 45%</li>
              <li className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 p-2.5"><Frown size={14} className="text-slate-400" /> User is frustrated / angry</li>
            </ul>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 bg-emerald-50/60 p-4">
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800"><GraduationCap size={14} /> Continuous learning</p>
            <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-emerald-700">Every time you reply as a human agent, that reply is captured into the AI's memory and used on future chats — so it keeps sounding more like your team.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Dialog open={newOpen} onOpenChange={setNewOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-md">
          <DialogHeader><DialogTitle>Simulate an incoming chat</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-3 py-2">
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Customer name</label>
              <input data-testid="new-chat-name" value={form.customer_name} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, customer_name: e.target.value })} placeholder="e.g. Rahul Gupta" className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-400" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">Marketplace</label>
              <Select value={form.marketplace} onValueChange={(v) => setForm({ ...form, marketplace: v })}>
                <SelectTrigger data-testid="new-chat-marketplace"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>{MKTS.map((m) => <SelectItem key={m} value={m}>{m}</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">First message</label>
              <input data-testid="new-chat-message" value={form.message} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, message: e.target.value })} className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-400" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <button onClick={() => setNewOpen(false)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50">Cancel</button>
            <button data-testid="create-chat-confirm" onClick={createChat} disabled={!form.customer_name.trim() || thinking} className="rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white disabled:opacity-50">Start chat</button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}


======================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/ai/CannedResponses.jsx
======================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { Pill, EmptyState } from "@/components/shared/UI";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { MessageSquareQuote, Plus, Pencil, Trash2, TrendingUp, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogFooter } from "@/components/ui/dialog";

const CATS = ["Refunds", "Verification", "Payments", "Rewards", "Technical", "General"];
const CHANNELS = ["both", "email", "chat"];
const empty = { title: "", category: "General", channel: "both", body: "" };

const CARD_FADE = { initial: { opacity: 0, y: 8 }, animate: { opacity: 1, y: 0 } };

export default function CannedResponses() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState([]);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(empty);

  const load = useCallback(async () => { const { data } = await api.getCanned(); setItems(data); }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const openNew = () => { setEditing(null); setForm(empty); setOpen(true); };
  const openEdit = (c) => { setEditing(c); setForm({ title: c.title, category: c.category, channel: c.channel, body: c.body }); setOpen(true); };

  const save = async () => {
    if (!form.title.trim() || !form.body.trim()) return;
    if (editing) { await api.updateCanned(editing.id, form); toast.success("Canned response updated — AI adopts your new wording"); }
    else { await api.createCanned(form); toast.success("Canned response added"); }
    setOpen(false); await load();
  };
  const remove = async (c) => { await api.deleteCanned(c.id); toast.success("Removed"); await load(); };

  return (
    <div className="h-full overflow-y-auto p-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
        <div className="mb-6 flex items-end justify-between">
          <div>
            <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900"><MessageSquareQuote size={20} className="text-indigo-600" /> Canned Responses</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500"><Sparkles size={12} className="text-indigo-500" /> Reusable replies the AI draws from. Update one and the AI instantly answers in your new words.</p>
          </div>
          <button data-testid="add-canned-btn" onClick={openNew} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white shadow-sm transition-transform hover:scale-[1.03]"><Plus size={16} /> New response</button>
        </div>

        {items.length === 0 && <EmptyState icon={MessageSquareQuote} title="No canned responses" subtitle="Add some for the AI to reuse." />}
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {items.map((c, i) => (
            <motion.div key={c.id} {...CARD_FADE} transition={{ delay: i * 0.03 }} data-testid="canned-row" className="group flex items-start gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm transition-shadow hover:shadow-md">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="mb-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">{c.title}</h3>
                  <Pill className="border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">{c.category}</Pill>
                  <Pill className="border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700">{c.channel}</Pill>
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-slate-400"><TrendingUp size={11} /> used {c.usage_count}×</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-500">{c.body}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-1 opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100">
                <button data-testid="edit-canned-btn" onClick={() => openEdit(c)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100"><Pencil size={13} /></button>
                <button data-testid="delete-canned-btn" onClick={() => remove(c)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-1.5 text-rose-500 hover:bg-rose-50"><Trash2 size={13} /></button>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
          <DialogHeader><DialogTitle>{editing ? "Edit canned response" : "New canned response"}</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-3 py-2">
            <input data-testid="canned-title-input" value={form.title} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, title: e.target.value })} placeholder="Title" className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-400" />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <select data-testid="canned-category-select" value={form.category} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, category: e.target.value })} className="flex-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-400">
                {CATS.map((c) => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
              </select>
              <select data-testid="canned-channel-select" value={form.channel} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, channel: e.target.value })} className="flex-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-400">
                {CHANNELS.map((c) => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
              </select>
            </div>
            <textarea data-testid="canned-body-input" value={form.body} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, body: e.target.value })} rows={5} placeholder="The reply text…" className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-400" />
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <button onClick={() => setOpen(false)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50">Cancel</button>
            <button data-testid="save-canned-btn" onClick={save} className="rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white">Save</button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/ai/KnowledgeBase.jsx
======================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { Pill, EmptyState } from "@/components/shared/UI";
import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/constants";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { BookOpen, Plus, Pencil, Trash2, FileText, Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogFooter } from "@/components/ui/dialog";

const CATS = ["Getting Started", "Rewards", "Refunds", "Verification", "Payments", "Trust & Safety", "Account", "Technical", "General"];
const CAT_COLOR = {
  "Getting Started": "border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700",
  Rewards: "border-violet-200 bg-violet-50 text-violet-700",
  Refunds: "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700",
  Verification: "border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700",
  Payments: "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-700",
  "Trust & Safety": "border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700",
  Account: "border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600",
  Technical: "border-cyan-200 bg-cyan-50 text-cyan-700",
  General: "border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600",
};

const empty = { title: "", category: "General", content: "" };

const CARD_FADE = { initial: { opacity: 0, y: 10 }, animate: { opacity: 1, y: 0 } };

export default function KnowledgeBase() {
  const [articles, setArticles] = useState([]);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(empty);

  const load = useCallback(async () => { const { data } = await api.getKbArticles(); setArticles(data); }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

  const openNew = () => { setEditing(null); setForm(empty); setOpen(true); };
  const openEdit = (a) => { setEditing(a); setForm({ title: a.title, category: a.category, content: a.content }); setOpen(true); };

  const save = async () => {
    if (!form.title.trim() || !form.content.trim()) return;
    if (editing) { await api.updateKb(editing.id, { ...form, tags: [] }); toast.success("Article updated — AI will use it instantly"); }
    else { await api.createKb({ ...form, tags: [] }); toast.success("Article added to the AI's knowledge"); }
    setOpen(false); await load();
  };
  const remove = async (a) => { await api.deleteKb(a.id); toast.success("Article removed"); await load(); };

  return (
    <div className="h-full overflow-y-auto p-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl">
        <div className="mb-6 flex items-end justify-between">
          <div>
            <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900"><BookOpen size={20} className="text-indigo-600" /> Knowledge Base</h2>
            <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500"><Sparkles size={12} className="text-indigo-500" /> The AI reads these on every conversation. Edit anything and it applies immediately.</p>
          </div>
          <button data-testid="add-kb-btn" onClick={openNew} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white shadow-sm transition-transform hover:scale-[1.03]"><Plus size={16} /> New article</button>
        </div>

        {articles.length === 0 && <EmptyState icon={FileText} title="No articles yet" subtitle="Add knowledge so the AI can answer accurately." />}
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {articles.map((a, i) => (
            <motion.div key={a.id} {...CARD_FADE} transition={{ delay: i * 0.03 }} data-testid="kb-article-card" className="group flex flex-col rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm transition-shadow hover:shadow-md">
              <div className="mb-2 flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                <Pill className={CAT_COLOR[a.category] || CAT_COLOR.General}>{a.category}</Pill>
                <div className="flex gap-1 opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100">
                  <button data-testid="edit-kb-btn" onClick={() => openEdit(a)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100"><Pencil size={13} /></button>
                  <button data-testid="delete-kb-btn" onClick={() => remove(a)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-1.5 text-rose-500 hover:bg-rose-50"><Trash2 size={13} /></button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">{a.title}</h3>
              <p className="mt-1.5 line-clamp-4 flex-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-500">{a.content}</p>
              <p className="mt-3 text-[10px] text-slate-400">Updated {timeAgo(a.updated_at)}{a.source === "CLAUDE.md" ? " · seeded" : ""}</p>
            </motion.div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
          <DialogHeader><DialogTitle>{editing ? "Edit article" : "New knowledge article"}</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-3 py-2">
            <input data-testid="kb-title-input" value={form.title} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, title: e.target.value })} placeholder="Title (e.g. Refund timelines)" className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-400" />
            <select data-testid="kb-category-select" value={form.category} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, category: e.target.value })} className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-400">
              {CATS.map((c) => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
            </select>
            <textarea data-testid="kb-content-input" value={form.content} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, content: e.target.value })} rows={6} placeholder="What should the AI know?" className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-400" />
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <button onClick={() => setOpen(false)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50">Cancel</button>
            <button data-testid="save-kb-btn" onClick={save} className="rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white">Save</button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: frontend/src/components/ai/TrainingInsights.jsx
======================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { api } from "@/lib/api";
import { StatCard } from "@/components/shared/UI";
import { timeAgo } from "@/lib/constants";
import {
  GraduationCap, BookOpen, MessageSquareQuote, Bot, ArrowRightLeft, Sparkles, MessageSquare, Mail, Hash,
} from "lucide-react";
import { BarChart, Bar, XAxis, YAxis, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip, Cell, PieChart, Pie } from "recharts";

const KW_TICK = { fontSize: 12, fill: "#64748b" };
const CHART_CURSOR = { fill: "#f1f5f9" };
const TOOLTIP_STYLE = { borderRadius: 12, border: "1px solid #e2e8f0", fontSize: 12 };

const ROW_FADE = { initial: { opacity: 0, x: -8 }, animate: { opacity: 1, x: 0 } };

export default function TrainingInsights() {
  const [d, setD] = useState(null);
  useEffect(() => { api.getTrainingInsights().then(({ data }) => setD(data)); }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  if (!d) return null;

  const kw = d.top_keywords.map((k) => ({ name: k.word, value: k.count }));
  const resRate = d.ai_chats > 0 ? Math.round((d.resolved_by_ai / d.ai_chats) * 100) : 0;
  const donut = [
    { name: "Resolved by AI", value: d.resolved_by_ai, color: "#4F46E5" },
    { name: "Handed to human", value: d.handoffs, color: "#E11D48" },
  ];
  const KW_COLORS = ["#4F46E5", "#7C3AED", "#2563EB", "#059669", "#D97706", "#0891B2"];

  return (
    <div className="h-full overflow-y-auto p-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl space-y-6">
        <div>
          <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900"><GraduationCap size={20} className="text-indigo-600" /> Training Insights</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500"><Sparkles size={12} className="text-indigo-500" /> What the AI has learned from your team, your docs, and real conversations.</p>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <StatCard icon={GraduationCap} label="Replies learned" value={d.examples_count} sub="from human agents" tone="bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600" accent="text-indigo-600" index={0} />
          <StatCard icon={BookOpen} label="Knowledge articles" value={d.kb_count} tone="bg-violet-50 text-violet-600" accent="text-violet-600" index={1} />
          <StatCard icon={MessageSquareQuote} label="Canned responses" value={d.canned_count} tone="bg-blue-50 text-blue-600" accent="text-blue-600" index={2} />
          <StatCard icon={Bot} label="AI resolution rate" value={`${resRate}%`} sub={`${d.resolved_by_ai}/${d.ai_chats} chats`} tone="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600" accent="text-emerald-600" index={3} />
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-12">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm lg:col-span-7">
            <p className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800"><Hash size={15} className="text-slate-500" /> Top user question keywords</p>
            <div className="h-64">
              {kw.length > 0 ? (
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <BarChart data={kw} layout="vertical" margin={{ left: 10 }}>
                    <XAxis type="number" hide />
                    <YAxis type="category" dataKey="name" tick={KW_TICK} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} width={90} />
                    <Tooltip cursor={CHART_CURSOR} contentStyle={TOOLTIP_STYLE} />
                    <Bar dataKey="value" radius={[0, 8, 8, 0]}>{kw.map((e, i) => <Cell key={e.name} fill={KW_COLORS[i % KW_COLORS.length]} />)}</Bar>
                  </BarChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              ) : <p className="pt-16 text-center text-sm text-slate-400">No questions learned yet.</p>}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm lg:col-span-5">
            <p className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800"><ArrowRightLeft size={15} className="text-slate-500" /> AI vs Human handoff</p>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="h-40 w-40">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <PieChart>
                    <Pie data={donut} dataKey="value" innerRadius={44} outerRadius={64} paddingAngle={3} stroke="none">
                      {donut.map((e) => <Cell key={e.name} fill={e.color} />)}
                    </Pie>
                    <Tooltip contentStyle={TOOLTIP_STYLE} />
                  </PieChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {donut.map((s) => (
                  <div key={s.name} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-600"><span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-sm" style={{ background: s.color }} /> {s.name}<span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{s.value}</span></div>
                ))}
                <div className="mt-2 border-t border-slate-100 pt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">Channels learned: <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{Object.entries(d.by_channel).map(([k, v]) => `${k} ${v}`).join(" · ") || "—"}</span></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm lg:col-span-7">
            <p className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800"><GraduationCap size={15} className="text-indigo-500" /> Recently learned agent replies</p>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {d.recent_examples.map((e, i) => (
                <motion.div key={`${e.channel}-${e.user_question}-${i}`} {...ROW_FADE} transition={{ delay: i * 0.04 }} data-testid="learned-example-row" className="rounded-xl border border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/60 p-3">
                  <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">{e.channel === "email" ? <Mail size={12} /> : <MessageSquare size={12} />} User asked: <span className="text-slate-700">“{e.user_question}”</span></p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-600"><span className="font-medium text-indigo-600">Learned reply:</span> {e.agent_reply}</p>
                </motion.div>
              ))}
              {d.recent_examples.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">No learned replies yet — reply to a chat or email as a human to teach the AI.</p>}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm lg:col-span-5">
            <p className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800"><MessageSquareQuote size={15} className="text-slate-500" /> Most-used canned responses</p>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {d.top_canned.map((c) => (
                <div key={c.title} data-testid="top-canned-row" className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="truncate text-xs text-slate-600">{c.title}</span>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <div className="h-1.5 w-24 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100"><div className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-violet-500" style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (c.usage_count / (d.top_canned[0]?.usage_count || 1)) * 100)}%` }} /></div>
                    <span className="w-6 text-right text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">{c.usage_count}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import { NavLink } from "react-router-dom";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { TEAMS, AI_NAV } from "@/lib/constants";
import { Sparkles } from "lucide-react";

export default function Sidebar() {
  return (
    <aside className="flex w-[248px] shrink-0 flex-col border-r border-slate-200 bg-white" data-testid="sidebar">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-6 py-6">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white shadow-sm">
          <span className="font-mono text-lg font-bold">F</span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <p className="text-base font-bold leading-none tracking-tight text-slate-900">Fayr</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">Ops Desk</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <nav className="flex-1 space-y-1 px-3 py-2">
        <p className="px-3 pb-2 pt-3 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">AI</p>
        <NavLink
          to={AI_NAV.path}
          data-testid="nav-ai"
          className={({ isActive }) =>
            `group relative flex items-center gap-3 overflow-hidden rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors ${
              isActive ? "text-white" : "text-indigo-700 hover:bg-indigo-50"
            }`
          }
        >
          {({ isActive }) => (
            <>
              {isActive && (
                <motion.span layoutId="active-pill" className="absolute inset-0 -z-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-violet-600" transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 400, damping: 32 }} />
              )}
              {!isActive && <span className="absolute inset-0 -z-10 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-50 to-violet-50" />}
              <AI_NAV.icon size={18} className={isActive ? "text-white" : "text-indigo-600"} />
              <span>{AI_NAV.label}</span>
              <span className={`ml-auto rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-bold uppercase ${isActive ? "bg-white/20 text-white" : "bg-indigo-600 text-white"}`}>New</span>
            </>
          )}
        </NavLink>

        <p className="px-3 pb-2 pt-4 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">Teams</p>
        {TEAMS.map((t) => {
          const Icon = t.icon;
          return (
            <NavLink
              key={t.key}
              to={t.path}
              data-testid={`nav-${t.key}`}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                `group relative flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                  isActive ? "bg-slate-900 text-white" : "text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100"
                }`
              }
            >
              {({ isActive }) => (
                <>
                  {isActive && (
                    <motion.span
                      layoutId="active-pill"
                      className="absolute inset-0 -z-10 rounded-xl bg-slate-900"
                      transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 400, damping: 32 }}
                    />
                  )}
                  <Icon size={18} className={isActive ? "text-white" : "text-slate-400 group-hover:text-slate-600"} />
                  <span>{t.label}</span>
                </>
              )}
            </NavLink>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      <div className="m-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-gradient-to-b from-slate-50 to-white p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-900">
          <Sparkles size={15} className="text-blue-600" />
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold">AI Assist enabled</p>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-slate-500">
          Reply suggestions & chat summaries powered by Claude across every conversation.
        </p>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}


======================================================================
FILE: frontend/src/components/layout/Topbar.jsx
======================================================================

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { useApp } from "@/context/AppContext";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/shared/UI";
import { PRESENCE_STYLE } from "@/lib/constants";
import { Search, ChevronDown, Command, LogOut } from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "@/context/AuthContext";
import {
  DropdownMenu, DropdownMenuContent, DropdownMenuItem, DropdownMenuTrigger, DropdownMenuLabel, DropdownMenuSeparator,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";

const PRESENCE_OPTIONS = ["online", "away", "busy", "offline"];

export default function Topbar() {
  const { currentAgent, agents, setCurrentAgent, changePresence } = useApp();
  const { logout } = useAuth();
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  if (!currentAgent) return <div className="h-[65px] border-b border-slate-200 bg-white" />;
  const p = PRESENCE_STYLE[currentAgent.presence];

  return (
    <header className="flex h-[65px] shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white px-8" data-testid="topbar">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-md">
        <Search size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
        <input
          data-testid="global-search-input"
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Search tickets, chats, users…"
          className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 py-2 pl-9 pr-16 text-sm text-slate-700 outline-none transition-colors focus:border-slate-300 focus:bg-white"
        />
        <span className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 flex -translate-y-1/2 items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-slate-200 bg-white px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-400">
          <Command size={10} /> K
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        {/* current user switcher */}
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <button data-testid="current-agent-switcher" className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white py-1.5 pl-1.5 pr-3 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50">
              <Avatar src={currentAgent.avatar} name={currentAgent.name} size={30} presence={currentAgent.presence} />
              <div className="text-left">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold leading-none text-slate-900">{currentAgent.name}</p>
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-[10px] text-slate-400">{currentAgent.role}</p>
              </div>
              <ChevronDown size={14} className="text-slate-400" />
            </button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="w-60">
            <DropdownMenuLabel className="text-xs">Switch acting agent</DropdownMenuLabel>
            <DropdownMenuSeparator />
            {agents.map((a) => (
              <DropdownMenuItem key={a.id} data-testid={`switch-agent-${a.id}`} onClick={() => setCurrentAgent(a)} className="gap-2">
                <Avatar src={a.avatar} name={a.name} size={24} presence={a.presence} />
                <span className="flex-1 text-xs">{a.name}</span>
                <span className="text-[10px] capitalize text-slate-400">{a.short || a.role}</span>
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            ))}
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>

        {/* presence toggle */}
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <button data-testid="agent-presence-status-toggle" className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50">
              <span className={`h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full ${p.dot}`} data-testid="agent-presence-indicator" />
              {p.label}
              <ChevronDown size={13} className="text-slate-400" />
            </button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="w-44">
            <DropdownMenuLabel className="text-xs">Set your status</DropdownMenuLabel>
            <DropdownMenuSeparator />
            {PRESENCE_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
              <DropdownMenuItem key={opt} data-testid={`presence-option-${opt}`} onClick={() => changePresence(opt)} className="gap-2 text-xs">
                <span className={`h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full ${PRESENCE_STYLE[opt].dot}`} />
                {PRESENCE_STYLE[opt].label}
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            ))}
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>

        <button data-testid="logout-btn" onClick={logout} title="Log out" className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600">
          <LogOut size={14} /> Logout
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import {
  MARKETPLACE_STYLE, PRIORITY_STYLE, STATUS_STYLE, SENTIMENT_STYLE, PRESENCE_STYLE, initials,
} from "@/lib/constants";

export const Pill = ({ className = "", children, ...props }) => (
  <span
    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium capitalize ${className}`}
    {...props}
  >
    {children}
  </span>
);

export const MarketplaceBadge = ({ name }) => (
  <Pill className={MARKETPLACE_STYLE[name] || "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border-slate-200"} data-testid="marketplace-badge">
    {name}
  </Pill>
);

export const PriorityBadge = ({ priority }) => (
  <Pill className={PRIORITY_STYLE[priority] || PRIORITY_STYLE.low} data-testid="ticket-priority-badge">
    {priority}
  </Pill>
);

export const StatusBadge = ({ status }) => (
  <Pill className={STATUS_STYLE[status] || "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border-slate-200"} data-testid="status-badge">
    {status}
  </Pill>
);

export const SentimentBadge = ({ sentiment }) => (
  <Pill className={SENTIMENT_STYLE[sentiment] || SENTIMENT_STYLE.neutral}>{sentiment}</Pill>
);

export const Avatar = ({ src, name, size = 36, presence }) => {
  const p = presence ? PRESENCE_STYLE[presence] : null;
  return (
    <div className="relative shrink-0" style={{ width: size, height: size }}>
      {src ? (
        <img
          src={src}
          alt={name}
          className="h-full w-full rounded-full object-cover ring-2 ring-white"
          onError={(e) => { e.target.style.display = "none"; e.target.nextSibling.style.display = "flex"; }}
        />
      ) : null}
      <div
        className="absolute inset-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600"
        style={{ display: src ? "none" : "flex" }}
      >
        {initials(name)}
      </div>
      {p && (
        <span className={`absolute -bottom-0.5 -right-0.5 h-3 w-3 rounded-full border-2 border-white ${p.dot}`} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

const CARD_INIT = { opacity: 0, y: 12 };
const CARD_ANIM = { opacity: 1, y: 0 };

export const StatCard = ({ icon: Icon, label, value, sub, accent = "text-slate-900", tone = "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600", index = 0 }) => (
  <motion.div
    initial={CARD_INIT}
    animate={CARD_ANIM}
    transition={{ duration: 0.35, delay: index * 0.05 }}
    className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm"
    data-testid="stat-card"
  >
    <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
      <div>
        <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">{label}</p>
        <p className={`mt-2 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight ${accent}`}>{value}</p>
        {sub && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">{sub}</p>}
      </div>
      {Icon && (
        <div className={`rounded-xl p-2.5 ${tone}`}>
          <Icon size={20} />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  </motion.div>
);

export const PageHeader = ({ title, subtitle, children }) => (
  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white px-8 py-6">
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900" data-testid="page-title">{title}</h1>
      {subtitle && <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p>}
    </div>
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">{children}</div>
  </div>
);

export const EmptyState = ({ icon: Icon, title, subtitle }) => (
  <div className="flex h-full min-h-[300px] flex-col items-center justify-center text-center">
    {Icon && <div className="mb-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 p-4 text-slate-400"><Icon size={28} /></div>}
    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">{title}</p>
    {subtitle && <p className="mt-1 max-w-xs text-xs text-slate-400">{subtitle}</p>}
  </div>
);

import * as React from "react"
import * as AccordionPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-accordion"
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const Accordion = AccordionPrimitive.Root

const AccordionItem = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AccordionPrimitive.Item ref={ref} className={cn("border-b", className)} {...props} />
))
AccordionItem.displayName = "AccordionItem"

const AccordionTrigger = React.forwardRef(({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AccordionPrimitive.Header className="flex">
    <AccordionPrimitive.Trigger
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-1 items-center justify-between py-4 text-sm font-medium transition-all hover:underline text-left [&[data-state=open]>svg]:rotate-180",
        className
      )}
      {...props}>
      {children}
      <ChevronDown
        className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground transition-transform duration-200" />
    </AccordionPrimitive.Trigger>
  </AccordionPrimitive.Header>
))
AccordionTrigger.displayName = AccordionPrimitive.Trigger.displayName

const AccordionContent = React.forwardRef(({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AccordionPrimitive.Content
    ref={ref}
    className="overflow-hidden text-sm data-[state=closed]:animate-accordion-up data-[state=open]:animate-accordion-down"
    {...props}>
    <div className={cn("pb-4 pt-0", className)}>{children}</div>
  </AccordionPrimitive.Content>
))
AccordionContent.displayName = AccordionPrimitive.Content.displayName

export { Accordion, AccordionItem, AccordionTrigger, AccordionContent }

import * as React from "react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const AlertDialog = AlertDialogPrimitive.Root

const AlertDialogTrigger = AlertDialogPrimitive.Trigger

const AlertDialogPortal = AlertDialogPrimitive.Portal

const AlertDialogOverlay = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay
    className={cn(
      "fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0",
      className
    )}
    {...props}
    ref={ref} />
))
AlertDialogOverlay.displayName = AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay.displayName

const AlertDialogContent = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AlertDialogPortal>
    <AlertDialogOverlay />
    <AlertDialogPrimitive.Content
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "fixed left-[50%] top-[50%] z-50 grid w-full max-w-lg translate-x-[-50%] translate-y-[-50%] gap-4 border bg-background p-6 shadow-lg duration-200 data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0 data-[state=closed]:zoom-out-95 data-[state=open]:zoom-in-95 data-[state=closed]:slide-out-to-left-1/2 data-[state=closed]:slide-out-to-top-[48%] data-[state=open]:slide-in-from-left-1/2 data-[state=open]:slide-in-from-top-[48%] sm:rounded-lg",
        className
      )}
      {...props} />
  </AlertDialogPortal>
))
AlertDialogContent.displayName = AlertDialogPrimitive.Content.displayName

  className,
  ...props

    className={cn("flex flex-col space-y-2 text-center sm:text-left", className)}

)
AlertDialogHeader.displayName = "AlertDialogHeader"

const AlertDialogFooter = ({
  className,

}) => (

))
AlertDialogTitle.displayName = AlertDialogPrimitive.Title.displayName

import * as React from "react"
import { cva } from "class-variance-authority";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const alertVariants = cva(
  "relative w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 text-sm [&>svg+div]:translate-y-[-3px] [&>svg]:absolute [&>svg]:left-4 [&>svg]:top-4 [&>svg]:text-foreground [&>svg~*]:pl-7",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-background text-foreground",
        destructive:
          "border-destructive/50 text-destructive dark:border-destructive [&>svg]:text-destructive",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

const Alert = React.forwardRef(({ className, variant, ...props }, ref) => (
  <div
    ref={ref}
    role="alert"
    className={cn(alertVariants({ variant }), className)}
    {...props} />
))
Alert.displayName = "Alert"

const AlertTitle = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <h5
    ref={ref}
    className={cn("mb-1 font-medium leading-none tracking-tight", className)}
    {...props} />
))
AlertTitle.displayName = "AlertTitle"

const AlertDescription = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <div
    ref={ref}
    className={cn("text-sm [&_p]:leading-relaxed", className)}
    {...props} />
))
AlertDescription.displayName = "AlertDescription"

export { Alert, AlertTitle, AlertDescription }

import * as React from "react"
import { cva } from "class-variance-authority";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const badgeVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center rounded-md border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring focus:ring-offset-2",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "border-transparent bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow hover:bg-primary/80",
        secondary:
          "border-transparent bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground hover:bg-secondary/80",
        destructive:
          "border-transparent bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow hover:bg-destructive/80",
        outline: "text-foreground",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

function Badge({
  className,
  variant,
  ...props
}) {
  return (<div className={cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className)} {...props} />);
}

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

  ({ ...props }, ref) => <nav ref={ref} aria-label="breadcrumb" {...props} />

    {...props} />

      ref={ref}

})
BreadcrumbLink.displayName = "BreadcrumbLink"

const BreadcrumbPage = React.forwardRef(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <span
    ref={ref}
    role="link"
    aria-disabled="true"
    aria-current="page"
    className={cn("font-normal text-foreground", className)}
    {...props} />
))
BreadcrumbPage.displayName = "BreadcrumbPage"

const BreadcrumbSeparator = ({
  children,
  className,
  ...props
}) => (
  <li
    role="presentation"
    aria-hidden="true"
    className={cn("[&>svg]:w-3.5 [&>svg]:h-3.5", className)}
    {...props}>
    {children ?? <ChevronRight />}
  </li>
)
BreadcrumbSeparator.displayName = "BreadcrumbSeparator"

const BreadcrumbEllipsis = ({
  className,
  ...props
}) => (
  <span
    role="presentation"
    aria-hidden="true"
    className={cn("flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center", className)}
    {...props}>
    <MoreHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
    <span className="sr-only">More</span>
  </span>
)

import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cva } from "class-variance-authority";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:size-4 [&_svg]:shrink-0",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow hover:bg-primary/90",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-destructive/90",
        outline:
          "border border-input shadow-sm hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        secondary:
          "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-secondary/80",
        ghost: "hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-9 px-4 py-2",
        sm: "h-8 rounded-md px-3 text-xs",
        lg: "h-10 rounded-md px-8",
        icon: "h-9 w-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

const Button = React.forwardRef(({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button"
  return (
    <Comp
      className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
      ref={ref}
      {...props} />
  );
})
Button.displayName = "Button"

export { Button, buttonVariants }
